\u0026quot;user@example.com\u0026quot;  --submit\nplaywright-cli drag e2 e8\n# drop files or data onto an element (from outside the page)\nplaywright-cli drop e4 --path=./image.png\nplaywright-cli drop e4 --data=\u0026quot;text/plain=hello world\u0026quot;\nplaywright-cli hover e4\nplaywright-cli select e9 \u0026quot;option-value\u0026quot;\nplaywright-cli upload ./document.pdf\nplaywright-cli check e12\nplaywright-cli uncheck e12\nplaywright-cli snapshot\n# search the snapshot for text or a regexp, returns matching nodes with surrounding context\nplaywright-cli find \u0026quot;Sign in\u0026quot;\nplaywright-cli find --regex \u0026quot;Sign (in|up)\u0026quot;\n# wrap the regexp in slashes to add flags, e.g. /i for case-insensitive\nplaywright-cli find --regex \u0026quot;/sign (in|up)/i\u0026quot;\nplaywright-cli eval \u0026quot;document.title\u0026quot;\nplaywright-cli eval \u0026quot;el =\u0026gt; el.textContent\u0026quot; e5\n# get element id, class, or any attribute not data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Use specific browser when creating session\nplaywright-cli open --browser=chrome\nplaywright-cli open --browser=firefox\nplaywright-cli open --browser=webkit\nplaywright-cli open --browser=msedge\n\n# Emulate a generic mobile device (Pixel 10 for Chromium, iPhone 17 for WebKit).\n# Prefer this when a mobile layout is acceptable: mobile pages are usually\n# lighter, so snapshots are smaller and cheaper.\nplaywright-cli open --mobile\nplaywright-cli open --device=\u0026quot;iPhone 15\u0026quot;\n\n# Use persistent profile (by default profile is in-memory)\nplaywright-cli open --persistent\n# Use persistent profile with custom directory\nplaywright-cli open --profile=/path/to/profile\n\n# Connect to browser via Playwright Extension\nplaywright-cli attach --extension=chrome\n\n# Connect to a running Chrome or Edge by channel name\nplaywright-cli attach --cdp=chrome\nplaywright-cli attach --cdp=msedge\n\n# Connect to a running browser via CDP endpoint\nplaywright-cli attach --cdp=http://localhost:9222\n\n# Start with config file\nplaywright-cli open --config=my-config.json\n\n# Close the browser\nplaywright-cli close\n# Detach from an attached browser (leaves the external browser running)\nplaywright-cli -s=msedge detach\n# Delete user data for the default session\nplaywright-cli delete-data\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Use specific browser when creating session\u003c/span\u003e\nplaywright-cli open Snapshot\u003c/span\u003e\n[Snapshot](.playwright-cli/page-2026-02-14T19-22-42-679Z.yml)\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYou can also take a snapshot on demand using data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# default - save to a file with timestamp-based name\nplaywright-cli snapshot\n\n# save to file, use when snapshot is a part of the workflow result\nplaywright-cli snapshot --filename=after-click.yaml\n\n# snapshot an element instead of the whole page\nplaywright-cli snapshot \u0026quot;#main\u0026quot;\n\n# limit snapshot depth for efficiency, take a partial snapshot afterwards\nplaywright-cli snapshot --depth=4\nplaywright-cli snapshot e34\n\n# include each element's bounding box as [box=x,y,width,height]\nplaywright-cli snapshot --boxes\n\n# search a large snapshot instead of capturing it all — returns matching nodes\n# with 3 lines of context around each match (like grep -C)\nplaywright-cli find \u0026quot;Add to cart\u0026quot;\nplaywright-cli find --regex \u0026quot;\\\\$[0-9]+\\\\.[0-9]{2}\u0026quot;\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e snapshot is a part of the workflow result\u003c/span\u003e\nplaywright-cli snapshot --filename=after-click.yaml\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e element's bounding box as [box=x,y,width,height]\u003c/span\u003e\nplaywright-cli snapshot --boxes\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e search a large highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# create new browser session named \u0026quot;mysession\u0026quot; with persistent profile\nplaywright-cli -s=mysession open example.com --persistent\n# same with manually specified profile directory (use when requested explicitly)\nplaywright-cli -s=mysession open example.com --profile=/path/to/profile\nplaywright-cli -s=mysession click e6\nplaywright-cli -s=mysession close  # stop a named browser\nplaywright-cli -s=mysession delete-data  # delete user data for persistent session\n\nplaywright-cli list\n# Close all browsers\nplaywright-cli close-all\n# Forcefully kill all browser processes\nplaywright-cli kill-all\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e create new browser session named \"mysession\" with persistent profile\u003c/span\u003e\nplaywright-cli -s=mysession open example.com --persistent\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e same with manually specified profile directory (use when requested explicitly)\u003c/span\u003e\nplaywright-cli -s=mysession open example.com --profile=/path/to/profile\nplaywright-cli -s=mysession click e6\nplaywright-cli -s=mysession close  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e stop a named browser\u003c/span\u003e\nplaywright-cli -s=mysession delete-data  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e delete user data for persistent session\u003c/span\u003e\n\nplaywright-cli list\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Close all browsers\u003c/span\u003e\nplaywright-cli close-all\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Forcefully kill all browser playwright-cli","description: Automate browser interactions, test web pages and work with Playwright tests.","allowed-tools: Bash(playwright-cli:*) Bash(npx:*) Bash(npm:*)","---","","# Browser Automation with playwright-cli","","## Quick start","","```bash","# open new browser","playwright-cli open","# navigate to a page","playwright-cli goto https://playwright.dev","# interact with the page using refs from the snapshot","playwright-cli click e15","playwright-cli type \"page.click\"","playwright-cli press Enter","# take a screenshot (rarely used, as snapshot is more common)","playwright-cli screenshot","# close the browser","playwright-cli close","```","","## Commands","","### Core","","```bash","playwright-cli open","# open and navigate right away","playwright-cli open https://example.com/","playwright-cli goto https://playwright.dev","playwright-cli type \"search query\"","playwright-cli click e3","playwright-cli dblclick e7","# --submit presses Enter after filling the element","playwright-cli fill e5 \"user@example.com\"  --submit","playwright-cli drag e2 e8","# drop files or data onto an element (from outside the page)","playwright-cli drop e4 --path=./image.png","playwright-cli drop e4 --data=\"text/plain=hello world\"","playwright-cli hover e4","playwright-cli select e9 \"option-value\"","playwright-cli upload ./document.pdf","playwright-cli check e12","playwright-cli uncheck e12","playwright-cli snapshot","# search the snapshot for text or a regexp, returns matching nodes with surrounding context","playwright-cli find \"Sign in\"","playwright-cli find --regex \"Sign (in|up)\"","# wrap the regexp in slashes to add flags, e.g. /i for case-insensitive","playwright-cli find --regex \"/sign (in|up)/i\"","playwright-cli eval \"document.title\"","playwright-cli eval \"el =\u003e el.textContent\" e5","# get element id, class, or any attribute not visible in the snapshot","playwright-cli eval \"el =\u003e el.id\" e5","playwright-cli eval \"el =\u003e el.getAttribute('data-testid')\" e5","playwright-cli dialog-accept","playwright-cli dialog-accept \"confirmation text\"","playwright-cli dialog-dismiss","playwright-cli resize 1920 1080","playwright-cli close","```","","### Navigation","","```bash","playwright-cli go-back","playwright-cli go-forward","playwright-cli reload","```","","### Keyboard","","```bash","playwright-cli press Enter","playwright-cli press ArrowDown","playwright-cli keydown Shift","playwright-cli keyup Shift","```","","### Mouse","","```bash","playwright-cli mousemove 150 300","playwright-cli mousedown","playwright-cli mousedown right","playwright-cli mouseup","playwright-cli mouseup right","playwright-cli mousewheel 0 100","```","","### Save as","","```bash","playwright-cli screenshot","playwright-cli screenshot e5","playwright-cli screenshot --filename=page.png","playwright-cli screenshot --hires","playwright-cli pdf --filename=page.pdf","```","","### Tabs","","```bash","playwright-cli tab-list","playwright-cli tab-new","playwright-cli tab-new https://example.com/page","playwright-cli tab-close","playwright-cli tab-close 2","playwright-cli tab-select 0","```","","### Storage","","```bash","playwright-cli state-save","playwright-cli state-save auth.json","playwright-cli state-load auth.json","","# Cookies","playwright-cli cookie-list","playwright-cli cookie-list --domain=example.com","playwright-cli cookie-get session_id","playwright-cli cookie-set session_id abc123","playwright-cli cookie-set session_id abc123 --domain=example.com --httpOnly --secure","playwright-cli cookie-delete session_id","playwright-cli cookie-clear","","# LocalStorage","playwright-cli localstorage-list","playwright-cli localstorage-get theme","playwright-cli localstorage-set theme dark","playwright-cli localstorage-delete theme","playwright-cli localstorage-clear","","# SessionStorage","playwright-cli sessionstorage-list","playwright-cli sessionstorage-get step","playwright-cli sessionstorage-set step 3","playwright-cli sessionstorage-delete step","playwright-cli sessionstorage-clear","```","","### Network","","```bash","playwright-cli route \"**/*.jpg\" --status=404","playwright-cli route \"https://api.example.com/**\" --body='{\"mock\": true}'","playwright-cli route-list","playwright-cli unroute \"**/*.jpg\"","playwright-cli unroute","```","","### DevTools","","```bash","playwright-cli console","playwright-cli console warning","playwright-cli requests","playwright-cli request 5","playwright-cli run-code \"async page =\u003e await page.context().grantPermissions(['geolocation'])\"","playwright-cli run-code --filename=script.js","playwright-cli tracing-start","playwright-cli tracing-stop","playwright-cli video-start video.webm","playwright-cli video-chapter \"Chapter Title\" --description=\"Details\" --duration=2000","playwright-cli video-stop","","# annotate each subsequent action (click, type, ...) with a callout naming the action and highlighting the target","playwright-cli video-show-actions --duration=600 --position=top-right","playwright-cli video-hide-actions","","# launch the dashboard for UI review / design feedback — user annotates the page, you receive the annotated screenshot, snapshot, and notes","playwright-cli show --annotate","","# generate a Playwright locator for an element from its ref or selector","playwright-cli generate-locator e5 --raw","","# show a persistent highlight overlay for an element, optionally with a custom style","playwright-cli highlight e5","playwright-cli highlight e5 --style=\"outline: 3px dashed red\"","# hide a single element highlight, or all page highlights when no target is given","playwright-cli highlight e5 --hide","playwright-cli highlight --hide","```","","## Raw output","","The global `--raw` option strips page status, generated code, and snapshot sections from the output, returning only the result value. Use it to pipe command output into other tools. Commands that don't produce output return nothing.","","```bash","playwright-cli --raw eval \"JSON.stringify(performance.timing)\" | jq '.loadEventEnd - .navigationStart'","playwright-cli --raw eval \"JSON.stringify([...document.querySelectorAll('a')].map(a =\u003e a.href))\" \u003e links.json","playwright-cli --raw snapshot \u003e before.yml","playwright-cli click e5","playwright-cli --raw snapshot \u003e after.yml","diff before.yml after.yml","TOKEN=$(playwright-cli --raw cookie-get session_id)","playwright-cli --raw localstorage-get theme","```","","For structured output wrapping every reply as JSON, pass --json","```bash","playwright-cli list --json","```","","## Open parameters","```bash","# Use specific browser when creating session","playwright-cli open --browser=chrome","playwright-cli open --browser=firefox","playwright-cli open --browser=webkit","playwright-cli open --browser=msedge","","# Emulate a generic mobile device (Pixel 10 for Chromium, iPhone 17 for WebKit).","# Prefer this when a mobile layout is acceptable: mobile pages are usually","# lighter, so snapshots are smaller and cheaper.","playwright-cli open --mobile","playwright-cli open --device=\"iPhone 15\"","","# Use persistent profile (by default profile is in-memory)","playwright-cli open --persistent","# Use persistent profile with custom directory","playwright-cli open --profile=/path/to/profile","","# Connect to browser via Playwright Extension","playwright-cli attach --extension=chrome","","# Connect to a running Chrome or Edge by channel name","playwright-cli attach --cdp=chrome","playwright-cli attach --cdp=msedge","","# Connect to a running browser via CDP endpoint","playwright-cli attach --cdp=http://localhost:9222","","# Start with config file","playwright-cli open --config=my-config.json","","# Close the browser","playwright-cli close","# Detach from an attached browser (leaves the external browser running)","playwright-cli -s=msedge detach","# Delete user data for the default session","playwright-cli delete-data","```","","## URLs with `\u0026` on Windows","","On Windows, `cmd.exe` and PowerShell treat `\u0026` as a command separator, so URLs with multiple query parameters get truncated before `playwright-cli` runs. Escape `\u0026` with `^\u0026` in `cmd.exe`, or use `--%` in PowerShell:","","```batch","playwright-cli goto \"https://example.com/?a=1^\u0026b=2\"","```","","```powershell","playwright-cli --% goto \"https://example.com/?a=1\u0026b=2\"","```","","## Snapshots","","After each command, playwright-cli provides a snapshot of the current browser state.","","```bash","\u003e playwright-cli goto https://example.com","### Page","- Page URL: https://example.com/","- Page Title: Example Domain","### Snapshot","[Snapshot](.playwright-cli/page-2026-02-14T19-22-42-679Z.yml)","```","","You can also take a snapshot on demand using `playwright-cli snapshot` command. All the options below can be combined as needed.","","```bash","# default - save to a file with timestamp-based name","playwright-cli snapshot","","# save to file, use when snapshot is a part of the workflow result","playwright-cli snapshot --filename=after-click.yaml","","# snapshot an element instead of the whole page","playwright-cli snapshot \"#main\"","","# limit snapshot depth for efficiency, take a partial snapshot afterwards","playwright-cli snapshot --depth=4","playwright-cli snapshot e34","","# include each element's bounding box as [box=x,y,width,height]","playwright-cli snapshot --boxes","","# search a large snapshot instead of capturing it all — returns matching nodes","# with 3 lines of context around each match (like grep -C)","playwright-cli find \"Add to cart\"","playwright-cli find --regex \"\\\\$[0-9]+\\\\.[0-9]{2}\"","```","","## Targeting elements","","By default, use refs from the snapshot to interact with page elements.","","```bash","# get snapshot with refs","playwright-cli snapshot","","# interact using a ref","playwright-cli click e15","```","","You can also use css selectors or Playwright locators.","","```bash","# css selector","playwright-cli click \"#main \u003e button.submit\"","","# role locator","playwright-cli click \"getByRole('button', { name: 'Submit' })\"","","# test id","playwright-cli click \"getByTestId('submit-button')\"","```","","## Browser Sessions","","```bash","# create new browser session named \"mysession\" with persistent profile","playwright-cli -s=mysession open example.com --persistent","# same with manually specified profile directory (use when requested explicitly)","playwright-cli -s=mysession open example.com --profile=/path/to/profile","playwright-cli -s=mysession click e6","playwright-cli -s=mysession close  # stop a named browser","playwright-cli -s=mysession delete-data  # delete user data for persistent session","","playwright-cli list","# Close all browsers","playwright-cli close-all","# Forcefully kill all browser processes","playwright-cli kill-all","```","","## Installation","","If global `playwright-cli` command is not available, try a local version via `npx playwright cli`:","","```bash","npx --no-install playwright --version","```","","When local version is available, use `npx playwright cli` in all commands. Otherwise, install `playwright-cli` as a global command:","","```bash","npm install -g @playwright/cli@latest","```","","## Example: Form submission","","```bash","playwright-cli open https://example.com/form","playwright-cli snapshot","","playwright-cli fill e1 \"user@example.com\"","playwright-cli fill e2 \"password123\"","playwright-cli click e3","playwright-cli snapshot","playwright-cli close","```","","## Example: Multi-tab workflow","","```bash","playwright-cli open https://example.com","playwright-cli tab-new https://example.com/other","playwright-cli tab-list","playwright-cli tab-select 0","playwright-cli snapshot","playwright-cli close","```","","## Example: Debugging with DevTools","","```bash","playwright-cli open https://example.com","playwright-cli click e4","playwright-cli fill e7 \"test\"","playwright-cli console","playwright-cli requests","playwright-cli close","```","","```bash","playwright-cli open https://example.com","playwright-cli tracing-start","playwright-cli click e4","playwright-cli fill e7 \"test\"","playwright-cli tracing-stop","playwright-cli close","```","","## Example: Interactive session","","Ask the user for UI review or design feedback. The user draws boxes on the live page and types comments; you receive the annotated screenshot, the snapshot of the marked region, and the user's notes. Use this whenever the user asks for \"UI review\", \"design feedback\", or to \"ask the user what they think / want / mean\":","","```bash","playwright-cli open https://example.com","playwright-cli show --annotate","```","","## Specific tasks","","* **Running and Debugging Playwright tests** [references/playwright-tests.md](references/playwright-tests.md)","* **Request mocking** [references/request-mocking.md](references/request-mocking.md)","* **Running Playwright code** [references/running-code.md](references/running-code.md)","* **Browser session management** [references/session-management.md](references/session-management.md)","* **Storage state (cookies, localStorage)** [references/storage-state.md](references/storage-state.md)","* **Test generation (plan / generate / heal)** [references/test-generation.md](references/test-generation.md)","* **Tracing** [references/tracing.md](references/tracing.md)","* **Video recording** [references/video-recording.md](references/video-recording.md)","* **Inspecting element attributes** <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Browser Automation with playwright-cli</h1><a id="user-content-browser-automation-with-playwright-cli" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Browser Automation with playwright-cli" href="#browser-automation-with-playwright-cli"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick start</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# open new browser
playwright-cli open
# navigate to a page
playwright-cli goto https://playwright.dev
# interact with the page using refs from the snapshot
playwright-cli click e15
playwright-cli type &quot;page.click&quot;
playwright-cli press Enter
# take a screenshot (rarely used, as snapshot is more common)
playwright-cli screenshot
# close the browser
playwright-cli close"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> open new browser</span>
playwright-cli open
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> navigate to a page</span>
playwright-cli goto https://playwright.dev
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> interact with the page using refs from the snapshot</span>
playwright-cli click e15
playwright-cli <span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>page.click<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
playwright-cli press Enter
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> take a screenshot (rarely used, as snapshot is more common)</span>
playwright-cli screenshot
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> close the browser</span>
playwright-cli close</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Commands</h2><a id="user-content-commands" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Commands" href="#commands"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Core</h3><a id="user-content-core" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Core" href="#core"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="playwright-cli open
# open and navigate right away
playwright-cli open https://example.com/
playwright-cli goto https://playwright.dev
playwright-cli type &quot;search query&quot;
playwright-cli click e3
playwright-cli dblclick e7
# --submit presses Enter after filling the element
playwright-cli fill e5 &quot;user@example.com&quot;  --submit
playwright-cli drag e2 e8
# drop files or data onto an element (from outside the page)
playwright-cli drop e4 --path=./image.png
playwright-cli drop e4 --data=&quot;text/plain=hello world&quot;
playwright-cli hover e4
playwright-cli select e9 &quot;option-value&quot;
playwright-cli upload ./document.pdf
playwright-cli check e12
playwright-cli uncheck e12
playwright-cli snapshot
# search the snapshot for text or a regexp, returns matching nodes with surrounding context
playwright-cli find &quot;Sign in&quot;
playwright-cli find --regex &quot;Sign (in|up)&quot;
# wrap the regexp in slashes to add flags, e.g. /i for case-insensitive
playwright-cli find --regex &quot;/sign (in|up)/i&quot;
playwright-cli eval &quot;document.title&quot;
playwright-cli eval &quot;el =&gt; el.textContent&quot; e5
# get element id, class, or any attribute not visible in the snapshot
playwright-cli eval &quot;el =&gt; el.id&quot; e5
playwright-cli eval &quot;el =&gt; el.getAttribute('data-testid')&quot; e5
playwright-cli dialog-accept
playwright-cli dialog-accept &quot;confirmation text&quot;
playwright-cli dialog-dismiss
playwright-cli resize 1920 1080
playwright-cli close"><pre>playwright-cli open
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> open and navigate right away</span>

# Cookies
playwright-cli cookie-list
playwright-cli cookie-list --domain=example.com
playwright-cli cookie-get session_id
playwright-cli cookie-set session_id abc123
playwright-cli cookie-set session_id abc123 --domain=example.com --httpOnly --secure
playwright-cli cookie-delete session_id
playwright-cli cookie-clear

# LocalStorage
playwright-cli localstorage-list

playwright-cli localstorage-set theme dark
playwright-cli localstorage-delete theme
playwright-cli localstorage-clear

# SessionStorage
playwright-cli sessionstorage-list
playwright-cli sessionstorage-get step
playwright-cli sessionstorage-set step 3
playwright-cli sessionstorage-delete step
playwright-cli sessionstorage-clear"><pre>playwright-cli state-save

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="playwright-cli console
playwright-cli console warning
playwright-cli requests
playwright-cli request 5
playwright-cli run-code &quot;async page =&gt; await page.context().grantPermissions(['geolocation'])&quot;
playwright-cli run-code --filename=script.js
playwright-cli tracing-start
playwright-cli tracing-stop
playwright-cli video-start video.webm
playwright-cli video-chapter &quot;Chapter Title&quot; --description=&quot;Details&quot; --duration=2000
playwright-cli video-stop

# annotate each subsequent action (click, type, ...) with a callout naming the action and highlighting the target
playwright-cli video-show-actions --duration=600 --position=top-right
playwright-cli video-hide-actions

# launch the dashboard for UI review / design feedback — user annotates the page, you receive the annotated screenshot, snapshot, and notes
playwright-cli show --annotate

# generate a Playwright locator for an element from its ref or selector
playwright-cli generate-locator e5 --raw

# show a persistent highlight overlay for an element, optionally with a custom style
playwright-cli highlight e5
playwright-cli highlight e5 --style=&quot;outline: 3px dashed red&quot;
# hide a single element highlight, or all page highlights when no target is given
playwright-cli highlight e5 --hide
playwright-cli highlight --hide"><pre>playwright-cli console

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Raw output</h2><a id="user-content-raw-output" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Raw output" href="#raw-output"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The global <code>--raw</code> option strips page status, generated code, and snapshot sections from the output, returning only the result value. Use it to pipe command output into other tools. Commands that don't produce output return nothing.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="playwright-cli --raw eval &quot;JSON.stringify(performance.timing)&quot; | jq '.loadEventEnd - .navigationStart'
playwright-cli --raw eval &quot;JSON.stringify([...document.querySelectorAll('a')].map(a =&gt; a.href))&quot; &gt; links.json
playwright-cli --raw snapshot &gt; before.yml
playwright-cli click e5
playwright-cli --raw snapshot &gt; after.yml
diff before.yml after.yml
TOKEN=$(playwright-cli --raw cookie-get session_id)
playwright-cli --raw localstorage-get theme"><pre>playwright-cli --raw <span class="pl-c1">eval</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>JSON.stringify(performance.timing)<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> jq <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>.loadEventEnd - .navigationStart<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>
playwright-cli --raw <span class="pl-c1">eval</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>JSON.stringify([...document.querySelectorAll('a')].map(a =&gt; a.href))<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> links.json
playwright-cli --raw snapshot <span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> before.yml
playwright-cli click e5
playwright-cli --raw snapshot <span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> after.yml
diff before.yml after.yml
TOKEN=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$(</span>playwright-cli --raw cookie-get session_id<span class="pl-pds">)</span></span>
playwright-cli --raw localstorage-get theme</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">For structured output wrapping every reply as JSON, pass --json</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Open parameters</h2><a id="user-content-open-parameters" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Open parameters" href="#open-parameters"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Use specific browser when creating session
playwright-cli open --browser=chrome
playwright-cli open --browser=firefox
playwright-cli open --browser=webkit
playwright-cli open --browser=msedge

# Emulate a generic mobile device (Pixel 10 for Chromium, iPhone 17 for WebKit).
# Prefer this when a mobile layout is acceptable: mobile pages are usually
# lighter, so snapshots are smaller and cheaper.
playwright-cli open --mobile
playwright-cli open --device=&quot;iPhone 15&quot;

# Use persistent profile (by default profile is in-memory)
playwright-cli open --persistent
# Use persistent profile with custom directory
playwright-cli open --profile=/path/to/profile

# Connect to browser via Playwright Extension
playwright-cli attach --extension=chrome

# Connect to a running Chrome or Edge by channel name
playwright-cli attach --cdp=chrome
playwright-cli attach --cdp=msedge

# Connect to a running browser via CDP endpoint
playwright-cli attach --cdp=http://localhost:9222

# Start with config file
playwright-cli open --config=my-config.json

# Close the browser
playwright-cli close
# Detach from an attached browser (leaves the external browser running)
playwright-cli -s=msedge detach
# Delete user data for the default session
playwright-cli delete-data"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Use specific browser when creating session</span>
playwright-cli open --browser=chrome
playwright-cli open --browser=firefox
playwright-cli open --browser=webkit
playwright-cli open --browser=msedge

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Emulate a generic mobile device (Pixel 10 for Chromium, iPhone 17 for WebKit).</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Prefer this when a mobile layout is acceptable: mobile pages are usually</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> lighter, so snapshots are smaller and cheaper.</span>
playwright-cli open --mobile
playwright-cli open --device=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>iPhone 15<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Use persistent profile (by default profile is in-memory)</span>
playwright-cli open --persistent
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Use persistent profile with custom directory</span>
playwright-cli open --profile=/path/to/profile

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Connect to browser via Playwright Extension</span>
playwright-cli attach --extension=chrome

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Connect to a running Chrome or Edge by channel name</span>
playwright-cli attach --cdp=chrome
playwright-cli attach --cdp=msedge

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Connect to a running browser via CDP endpoint</span>
playwright-cli attach --cdp=http://localhost:9222

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Start with config file</span>
playwright-cli open --config=my-config.json

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Close the browser</span>
playwright-cli close
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Detach from an attached browser (leaves the external browser running)</span>
playwright-cli -s=msedge detach
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Delete user data for the default session</span>
playwright-cli delete-data</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">URLs with <code>&amp;</code> on Windows</h2><a id="user-content-urls-with--on-windows" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: URLs with &amp; on Windows" href="#urls-with--on-windows"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">On Windows, <code>cmd.exe</code> and PowerShell treat <code>&amp;</code> as a command separator, so URLs with multiple query parameters get truncated before <code>playwright-cli</code> runs. Escape <code>&amp;</code> with <code>^&amp;</code> in <code>cmd.exe</code>, or use <code>--%</code> in PowerShell:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-batchfile notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="playwright-cli goto &quot;https://example.com/?a=1^&amp;b=2&quot;"><pre>playwright-cli <span class="pl-k">goto</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>https://example.com/?a=1^&amp;b=2<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="playwright-cli --% goto &quot;https://example.com/?a=1&amp;b=2&quot;"><pre>playwright<span class="pl-k">-</span>cli <span class="pl-k">--%</span><span class="pl-s"> goto "https://example.com/?a=1&amp;b=2"</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Snapshots</h2><a id="user-content-snapshots" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Snapshots" href="#snapshots"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">After each command, playwright-cli provides a snapshot of the current browser state.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&gt; playwright-cli goto https://example.com
### Page
- Page URL: https://example.com/
- Page Title: Example Domain
### Snapshot
[Snapshot](.playwright-cli/page-2026-02-14T19-22-42-679Z.yml)"><pre><span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> playwright-cli goto https://example.com
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span>## Page</span>
- Page URL: https://example.com/
- Page Title: Example Domain
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span>## Snapshot</span>
[Snapshot](.playwright-cli/page-2026-02-14T19-22-42-679Z.yml)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You can also take a snapshot on demand using <code>playwright-cli snapshot</code> command. All the options below can be combined as needed.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# default - save to a file with timestamp-based name
playwright-cli snapshot

# save to file, use when snapshot is a part of the workflow result
playwright-cli snapshot --filename=after-click.yaml

# snapshot an element instead of the whole page
playwright-cli snapshot &quot;#main&quot;

# limit snapshot depth for efficiency, take a partial snapshot afterwards
playwright-cli snapshot --depth=4
playwright-cli snapshot e34

# include each element's bounding box as [box=x,y,width,height]
playwright-cli snapshot --boxes

# search a large snapshot instead of capturing it all — returns matching nodes
# with 3 lines of context around each match (like grep -C)
playwright-cli find &quot;Add to cart&quot;
playwright-cli find --regex &quot;\\$[0-9]+\\.[0-9]{2}&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> default - save to a file with timestamp-based name</span>
playwright-cli snapshot

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> save to file, use when snapshot is a part of the workflow result</span>
playwright-cli snapshot --filename=after-click.yaml

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> snapshot an element instead of the whole page</span>
playwright-cli snapshot <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>#main<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> limit snapshot depth for efficiency, take a partial snapshot afterwards</span>
playwright-cli snapshot --depth=4
playwright-cli snapshot e34

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> include each element's bounding box as [box=x,y,width,height]</span>
playwright-cli snapshot --boxes

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> search a large snapshot instead of capturing it all — returns matching nodes</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> with 3 lines of context around each match (like grep -C)</span>
playwright-cli find <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Add to cart<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
playwright-cli find --regex <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-cce">\\</span>$[0-9]+<span class="pl-cce">\\</span>.[0-9]{2}<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Targeting elements</h2><a id="user-content-targeting-elements" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Targeting elements" href="#targeting-elements"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">By default, use refs from the snapshot to interact with page elements.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# get snapshot with refs
playwright-cli snapshot

# interact using a ref
playwright-cli click e15"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> get snapshot with refs</span>
playwright-cli snapshot

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> interact using a ref</span>
playwright-cli click e15</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You can also use css selectors or Playwright locators.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# css selector
playwright-cli click &quot;#main &gt; button.submit&quot;

# role locator
playwright-cli click &quot;getByRole('button', { name: 'Submit' })&quot;

# test id
playwright-cli click &quot;getByTestId('submit-button')&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> css selector</span>
playwright-cli click <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>#main &gt; button.submit<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> role locator</span>
playwright-cli click <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>getByRole('button', { name: 'Submit' })<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> test id</span>
playwright-cli click <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>getByTestId('submit-button')<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Browser Sessions</h2><a id="user-content-browser-sessions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Browser Sessions" href="#browser-sessions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# create new browser session named &quot;mysession&quot; with persistent profile
playwright-cli -s=mysession open example.com --persistent
# same with manually specified profile directory (use when requested explicitly)
playwright-cli -s=mysession open example.com --profile=/path/to/profile
playwright-cli -s=mysession click e6
playwright-cli -s=mysession close  # stop a named browser
playwright-cli -s=mysession delete-data  # delete user data for persistent session

playwright-cli list
# Close all browsers
playwright-cli close-all
# Forcefully kill all browser processes
playwright-cli kill-all"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> create new browser session named "mysession" with persistent profile</span>
playwright-cli -s=mysession open example.com --persistent
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> same with manually specified profile directory (use when requested explicitly)</span>
playwright-cli -s=mysession open example.com --profile=/path/to/profile
playwright-cli -s=mysession click e6
playwright-cli -s=mysession close  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> stop a named browser</span>
playwright-cli -s=mysession delete-data  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> delete user data for persistent session</span>

playwright-cli list
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Close all browsers</span>
playwright-cli close-all
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Forcefully kill all browser processes</span>
playwright-cli kill-all</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h2><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If global <code>playwright-cli</code> command is not available, try a local version via <code>npx playwright cli</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npx --no-install playwright --version"><pre>npx --no-install playwright --version</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">When local version is available, use <code>npx playwright cli</code> in all commands. Otherwise, install <code>playwright-cli</code> as a global command:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm install -g @playwright/cli@latest"><pre>npm install -g @playwright/cli@latest</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example: Form submission</h2><a id="user-content-example-form-submission" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example: Form submission" href="#example-form-submission"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="playwright-cli open https://example.com/form
playwright-cli snapshot

playwright-cli fill e1 &quot;user@example.com&quot;
playwright-cli fill e2 &quot;password123&quot;
playwright-cli click e3
playwright-cli snapshot
playwright-cli close"><pre>playwright-cli open https://example.com/form
playwright-cli snapshot

playwright-cli fill e1 <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>user@example.com<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
playwright-cli fill e2 <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>password123<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
playwright-cli click e3
playwright-cli snapshot
playwright-cli close</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example: Multi-tab workflow</h2><a id="user-content-example-multi-tab-workflow" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example: Multi-tab workflow" href="#example-multi-tab-workflow"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="playwright-cli open https://example.com

playwright-cli tab-list

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example: Debugging with DevTools</h2><a id="user-content-example-debugging-with-devtools" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example: Debugging with DevTools" href="#example-debugging-with-devtools"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="playwright-cli open https://example.com
playwright-cli click e4
playwright-cli fill e7 &quot;test&quot;
playwright-cli console
playwright-cli requests
playwright-cli close"><pre>playwright-cli open https://example.com
playwright-cli click e4
playwright-cli fill e7 <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>test<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
playwright-cli console
playwright-cli requests
playwright-cli close</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="playwright-cli open https://example.com
playwright-cli tracing-start
playwright-cli click e4
playwright-cli fill e7 &quot;test&quot;
playwright-cli tracing-stop
playwright-cli close"><pre>playwright-cli open https://example.com
playwright-cli tracing-start

playwright-cli tracing-stop
playwright-cli close</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example: Interactive session</h2><a id="user-content-example-interactive-session" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example: Interactive session" href="#example-interactive-session"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Ask the user for UI review or design feedback. The user draws boxes on the live page and types comments; you receive the annotated screenshot, the snapshot of the marked region, and the user's notes. Use this whenever the user asks for "UI review", "design feedback", or to "ask the user what they think / want / mean":</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="playwright-cli open https://example.com
playwright-cli show --annotate"><pre>playwright-cli open https://example.com
playwright-cli show --annotate</pre></div>
